## `AFG-8530-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** SPF-Animal-Microbiology Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for University Radioisotope Radiation Activity & Chemical Effluent
- **Intro:** SPF-Animal-Microbiology Metrologist — Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for University Radioisotope Radiation Activity & Chemical Effluent — Verify Batch Documentation and Chain-of-Custody: Declared activity must be within ±5% of the certificate value.
